- **ua-to-ga4**: Universal Analytics (UA) and GA4 use different names for equivalent concepts. UA was sunset on 2023-07-01; models trained before or around then guess UA field names that no longer exist in the GA4 Data API. On 2024-05-06 GA4 also renamed "conversions" to "key events" (see the conversions rows below). If a name feels obviously right but returns "Invalid metric/dimension", assume your training predates the change and verify with `search_schema`. This skill gives the correct GA4 Data API name for every common UA metric and dimension. ([surendranb/google-analytics-mcp](https://github.com/surendranb/google-analytics-mcp/tree/HEAD/skills/ua-to-ga4) · ★242)
